# Range sweep share links

Each cell's score as a toy-midi share link, generated with `pnpm cli render <score> <output>.link` from `packages/score-tool`. The flat performance, without the phrasing pass, with every voice on its own track and the whole piece looped.

toy-midi keeps one tempo per project, so cells whose score changes tempo play at their first tempo throughout: d04d, m02, m03, m04, o01, o02, o03, and s05, most of them a closing ritardando and s05 a tempo build.
